*Zhangchi Zhu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TF-LLMER提出了一种训练友好的LLM增强推荐框架，通过归一化和推荐感知的降维方法解决现有方法中存在的优化困难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM增强推荐方法将LLM表示注入骨干推荐器，以利用丰富的物品文本信息而不增加推理成本。但研究发现这些方法严重阻碍了骨干模型的优化，导致训练损失难以降低。

Method: 提出TF-LLMER框架，包含两个关键组件：1) 物品嵌入归一化以消除范数驱动的优化不稳定性；2) Rec-PCA方法，一种推荐感知的降维方法，通过注入协作结构解决语义-协作角度聚类错位问题。

Result: 理论和大量实验证明TF-LLMER显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: TF-LLMER通过解决LLM表示中的范数差异和语义-协作角度聚类错位问题，实现了训练友好的LLM增强推荐，在保持语义信息的同时更好地对齐协作结构。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-enhanced recommendation models inject LLM representations into backbone recommenders to exploit rich item text without inference-time LLM cost. However, we find that existing LLM-enhanced methods significantly hinder the optimization of backbone models, resulting in high training losses that are difficult to reduce. To address it, we establish a comprehensive theoretical analysis of local optimization curvature and identify two key causes: 1) large norm disparity and 2) semantic-collaboration misaligned angular clustering of LLM representations. Guided by these insights, we propose Training-Friendly LLM-Enhanced Recommender (TF-LLMER), a lightweight framework with two key components. First, we highlight the necessity of item embedding normalization to eliminate norm-driven instability and achieve provable control over optimization conditioning. Second, we introduce Rec-PCA, a recommendation-aware dimensionality reduction method that injects collaborative structure into the representation transformation to resolve semantic-collaboration misaligned angular clustering. It jointly optimizes semantic information retention and alignment with an item-item co-occurrence graph constructed from interaction histories. The graph captures collaborative structure, and alignment is promoted by penalizing total variation over the graph. Both theory and extensive experiments demonstrate that TF-LLMER significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/woriazzc/TF-LLMER.

</details>


### [190] [Coverage, Not Averages: Semantic Stratification for Trustworthy Retrieval Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20763)
*Andrew Klearman,Radu Revutchi,Rohin Garg,Rishav Chakravarti,Samuel Marc Denton,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于语义分层的检索评估框架，通过将文档组织到基于实体的可解释全局空间中，并系统地为缺失的语义层生成查询，从而提供形式化的语义覆盖保证和检索失败模式的可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前检索增强生成(RAG)中检索质量是准确性和鲁棒性的主要瓶颈。现有评估方法依赖启发式构建的查询集，引入了隐藏的内在偏差。作者将检索评估形式化为统计估计问题，发现指标可靠性从根本上受限于评估集构建方式。

Method: 提出语义分层方法：1) 将文档组织到基于实体的可解释全局空间中形成聚类；2) 系统地为缺失的语义层生成查询；3) 提供形式化的语义覆盖保证；4) 提供检索失败模式的可解释性分析。

Result: 在多个基准测试和检索方法上的实验验证了该框架的有效性。结果显示：1) 暴露了系统性的覆盖差距；2) 识别了解释检索性能方差的结构性信号；3) 分层评估比聚合指标提供更稳定、透明的评估；4) 支持更可信的决策制定。

Conclusion: 语义分层评估框架通过将评估建立在语料库结构基础上，提供了形式化的语义覆盖保证和可解释的检索失败分析，相比传统启发式评估方法，能够提供更可靠、透明和可信的检索系统评估。

Abstract: Retrieval quality is the primary bottleneck for accuracy and robustness in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Current evaluation relies on heuristically constructed query sets, which introduce a hidden intrinsic bias. We formalize retrieval evaluation as a statistical estimation problem, showing that metric reliability is fundamentally limited by the evaluation-set construction. We further introduce \emph{semantic stratification}, which grounds evaluation in corpus structure by organizing documents into an interpretable global space of entity-based clusters and systematically generating queries for missing strata. This yields (1) formal semantic coverage guarantees across retrieval regimes and (2) interpretable visibility into retrieval failure modes.
  Experiments across multiple benchmarks and retrieval methods validate our framework. The results expose systematic coverage gaps, identify structural signals that explain variance in retrieval performance, and show that stratified evaluation yields more stable and transparent assessments while supporting more trustworthy decision-making than aggregate metrics.

</details>
